MESH_IMPORT
{
	name "ufofy_0"
	mesh_gen model DIFF_EXPLODE {
		material "metal"
		mesh_gen UNION {
			mesh_gen BOX {
				min { 0.0 -0.5 0.0 }
				max { 1.0 0.0 1.0 }
			}
			mesh_gen CYLINDER {
				from { 0.5 -0.05 0.1 }
				to { 0.5 -0.05 0.9 }
				radius 0.4
				scale { 1.0 0.2 1.0 }
			}
		}
		mesh_gen BOX {
			min { -1.0 -2.0 -1.0 }
			max { 2.0 -0.1 2.0 }
		}
	}
}

				max { 1.0 0.0 1.0 }
			}
			mesh_gen BOX {
				min { 0.1 -0.1 0.1 }
				max { 0.2 0.1 0.9 }
			}
			mesh_gen BOX {
				min { 0.8 -0.1 0.1 }
				max { 0.9 0.1 0.9 }
			}
		}
		mesh_gen BOX {
			min { -1.0 -2.0 -1.0 }
			max { 2.0 -0.1 2.0 }
		}
	}
}
MESH_IMPORT
{
	name "ufofy_2"
	mesh_gen model DIFF_EXPLODE {
		material "metal"
		mesh_gen UNION {
			mesh_gen BOX {
				min { 0.0 -0.5 0.0 }
				max { 1.0 0.0 1.0 }
			}
			mesh_gen SPHERE {
				center { 0.2 -0.01 0.2 }
				radius 0.1
				scale { 1.0 0.2 1.0 }
			}
			mesh_gen SPHERE {
				center { 0.2 -0.01 0.8 }
				radius 0.1
				scale { 1.0 0.2 1.0 }
			}
			mesh_gen SPHERE {
				center { 0.8 -0.01 0.8 }
				radius 0.1
				scale { 1.0 0.2 1.0 }
			}
			mesh_gen SPHERE {
